# Wardogs: Challenges and Session Events

*Source: the `WDChallengeDefinition` tables listed in section 3, all 95 `StateTree` assets (`ST_*.json`), the five `DT_ChallengeRewards_SessionEvents_*` formula tables and the 16 `DT_RewardLookup_SessionEvent_*` lookup tables, all under [`data/Wardogs`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/tree/main/data/Wardogs). The plugin that holds most of them is [`Challenges_KOTH`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Challenges_KOTH/Content/Challenges_KOTH.json). Every table below is generated by [`_tools/challenge_tables.py`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/_tools/challenge_tables.py); do not edit it by hand.*

A challenge is a small rule that waits for one gameplay event and then pays out (or counts progress). This page lists every challenge, the event it waits for, what its state tree does, and which reward row it feeds. The amounts and formulas are on [Session-Event Reward Lookups](../content/Wardogs_RewardLookup_SessionEvents_v1.md); the live curves that scale some rewards (`LiveCurves.*`) are decoded on [Progression Tables](Wardogs_Progression_v1.md); the per-structure build rewards are on [Buildables (inventory)](Wardogs_Buildables_v1.md).

Labels: **Data** is read straight from the export. **Native** is read from the game code (nothing on this page needed it). **Derived** is worked out from Data values. "Not traced" means nobody has followed it yet.

## 1. Totals

<!-- challenge_tables counts -->
- StateTree assets: **95** (**60** under [`Challenges_KOTH`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Challenges_KOTH/Content/Challenges_KOTH.json), **79** named `ST_Challenges_SessionEvents_*`).
- Files under the [`Challenges_KOTH`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Challenges_KOTH/Content/Challenges_KOTH.json) plugin content: **75** (60 trees, 13 reward lookup tables, 1 challenge table, 1 feature file).
- Challenge definitions: **108** rows in **9** tables; **108** point at a StateTree.
- Trees that name a reward tag or a lookup registry: **72**.
- Across all trees: **1481** states, **2724** nodes (**857** conditions, **1867** tasks and evaluators).
- Reward formula rows available to join: **479** (in 5 tables); reward lookup tables: **16**.
<!-- /challenge_tables -->

## 2. How one challenge is put together

- **Data.** A challenge definition row (type `WDChallengeDefinition`) has a tag, a UI name and description, an auto-activate flag, a prerequisite list, a list of objectives (a tag plus a target number), a list of direct rewards, and a `StateTreeReference` that names one StateTree asset plus its parameter names. Rows sit in nine tables (listed in section 3).
- **Data.** The StateTree is the rule itself. In the session-event trees the shape is the same each time: a root state, a state that waits for one event (`Wait For ... Event`), a state that fires on it (`On ... Event`), a `Populate Context` state, then one branch per player who is paid (`On Killer`, `On Supply Crate Owner`, `On Player Instigator` and so on). Inside each branch the states add context tags, work out a reward and grant it. A last state, `Complete When Done`, ends the run. Example, [`ST_Challenges_SessionEvents_KOTH_DestroySupplyCrate`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Challenges_KOTH/Content/ST_Challenges_SessionEvents_KOTH_DestroySupplyCrate.json): `Wait For Destroyed Supply Crate Event`, `On Destroyed Supply Crate Event`, `Populate Context`, `On Supply Crate Owner` (add tag, `Calculate Reward`, `Grant Reward`), `On Killer` (role XP tag, crate item tag, zone and friendly-fire tags, `Get Supply Crate Variant Reward Tag`, `Calculate Reward`, `Update Reward XP Tags`, `Grant Reward`), `Complete When Done`.
- **Derived.** The export keeps node display names but drops the node class. A node with an `Operand` field is a condition, and any other node is a task or evaluator. The tables below count them that way.
- **Data.** Per-node settings live in the instance data: the reward tag a `Calculate Reward` or `Grant Reward` node uses, the calculator input names, the registry a `Lookup Reward Tag for Variant` node reads, cooldowns, delays and stat updates.
- **Derived.** The reward formula for a tag is the row of that name in a `DT_ChallengeRewards_SessionEvents_*` table. Sections 6 and 7 join the trees to those rows by tag name. Whether the game does the same join is not traced.

## 3. Challenge definitions

Every row of every table whose row type is `WDChallengeDefinition`. "Objective target" is the number the objective needs; "Direct reward" is the fixed payout in the row itself (only the passive missions use it). Session-event rows have neither, because their payout comes from the state tree and the formula tables.

<!-- /challenge_tables -->

Notes on the table above:

- **Data.** 60 of the 108 rows are KOTH session events. The other 48 are the buildable, crowbar, spotting, team-offence and hot-zone-magnet session events (19), achievements (10), passive missions (15) and wagers (4).
- **Data.** The `PlayerKill` row lists its own tag as a prerequisite with the invert flag set. What the engine does with a self-referencing, inverted prerequisite is not traced.
- **Data.** Passive missions pay in `Attribute.Meta.Currency.Intel`, not cash. `TimePlayed` targets are in seconds (1,800 for tier 1). `EarnRoleXp` has the same target (500) in all five tiers as exported; only the payout grows. Read the exact tiers in the table.
- **Data.** Wager challenges have no objective or reward in the row. Their target is a tree parameter (`NumPlayersToKill`, `NumPassengersToDeliver`, `NumPlayersToRevive`, `NumVehiclesToDestroy`). The value stored with each row is only the placeholder 1 (read from the package bytes, round 36); the real target is the count chosen on the wager slip, with limits and odds in [`DT_WagerActions`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Content/Meta/Wagers/DT_WagerActions.json) and the prebuilt objectives in [`DT_WagerObjectiveCatalog`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Content/Meta/Wagers/DT_WagerObjectiveCatalog.json) (rows in [`economy.json`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/assets/data/economy.json), method in [round 36 economy](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/docs/evidence/round36-econ.md)).
- **Data.** Achievement rows ([`DT_Challenges_SessionEvents_Shared`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Content/Meta/Challenges/DT_Challenges_SessionEvents_Shared.json)) have no objective number and no reward. Their thresholds are tree parameters whose values are not in the JSON export (CUE4Parse drops property-bag values); read from the package bytes in round 36: Loadout Spender `CashThreshold` 100,000, Single Life Profit `CashThreshold` 100,000, Control Zone Survivor `RequiredSeconds` 3,600, Long Shot `DistanceThreshold` 95,000 (Unreal units, so 950 m). The Steam achievement ids are in [`DT_Achievements`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Content/Meta/Achievements/DT_Achievements.json).

## 4. Which event each tree waits for

For each tree: the challenge row that uses it, the gameplay event tag it listens for (with the message type the event carries), the `On ...` branches, and the fixed settings found in its instance data (cooldowns, delays, stat updates, XP fallbacks, team-attitude filters). **Data**, except the branch list, which is **Derived** from state names. Event tags are `Event.StateTree.*`; the code that raises each event is not traced.

<!-- /challenge_tables -->

What the table shows:

- **Derived.** The 60 KOTH trees are 23 base events plus assist variants: 8 each for logistic, revive and supply-crate assists, 6 for crew assists, 4 for FOB-supplied assists and 3 plain `_Assist` trees. Every variant listens for its own event tag. That the game raises several of them for one kill is not traced.
- **Data.** Four wager trees (`Rewarded...`, `PassengersDelivered`) listen for `Event.StateTree.RewardGranted` and add 1 to a progress counter. Eight trees (kill, assist, vehicle, section, heal, revive) contain a `Broadcast Event` node whose event tag is the tree's own event.
- **Data.** Counts and targets for session events are not in the trees; the amounts are in the formula tables. Only the mission trees ([`ST_Challenge_EarnResource`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Content/Meta/Missions/StateTrees/ST_Challenge_EarnResource.json), [`ST_Challenge_TimePlayed`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Content/Meta/Missions/StateTrees/ST_Challenge_TimePlayed.json)) and the wager trees count towards a target.
- **Data.** "Repeat cooldown 0 s" comes from the `Get Repeat Actions Cooldown` node, which uses a per-victim or per-target tracker. The cooldown stored in the tree is 0 everywhere; the tracker types are in the "Tree parameters" column of section 3 (for example `VictimTracker` on `PlayerKill`). How the tracker lowers repeated rewards (the `LastVictimCount` terms in the formulas) is not traced in code. What is read (**Native**, round 85): the calculator builds its variable map in one function (`0x861DB00`) that registers `DistanceToTarget`, `LastVictimCount` and `KillStreak`, each with the value 0, so a tree that never sets `LastVictimCount` pays with a count of 0 (no reduction). The tasks that set it (`Track Target Repeat Count`, `Resolve Repeat Action Count`, class `WDSessionEventsResolveRepeatActionCountTask`) were not read.

## 5. Conditions

The conditions each tree evaluates and the context tags it adds or tests. `Challenges.Context.*` tags are labels the tree attaches to the event (for example `Challenges.Context.Target.Friendly`, `Challenges.Context.Instigator.InControlZone`, `Challenges.Context.Instigator.InHotZone`). The formula tables choose a variable by those labels. **Data**.

<!-- /challenge_tables -->

- **Derived.** The common conditions are `Object Equals` and `Tag Equal Condition` (compare two things or two tags), `Actor In Control Zone`, `Actor In Hot Zone`, `Actor Hostility Condition` (team attitude) and `Is Actor In Instigators Crew Condition`.
- **Derived.** The zone conditions are how a payout learns that the player or the victim stood in a control zone or a hot zone. The zone factors themselves are in the formulas ([reward lookup page, section 17](../content/Wardogs_RewardLookup_SessionEvents_v1.md)).

## 6. Rewards each tree feeds

For each tree, the reward tags it names in its instance data. **Step** is `calculate` (a `Calculate Reward` node, which also lists its input names), `grant` (a `Grant Reward` node) or `tip` (a tip tag). The next columns join the tag to the formula row of the same name: which table holds it, and the literal base values (cash `BaseReward` and similar, role XP `DefaultReward` and similar). Variables that are runtime values (kill streak, crew count, curves) are not literals and are left out. **Data** for each side, **Derived** for the join.

Reward tags a tree names that have no row in any of the five formula tables (44 tree and tag pairs): 18 `Challenges.Rewards.*` tags (`Challenges.Rewards.Assist.VehicleDestroyed`, `Challenges.Rewards.Assist.VehicleDestroyed.DamageAssist`, `Challenges.Rewards.Buildables.BuildSuppliesDeposited`, `Challenges.Rewards.DestroySupplyCrate`, `Challenges.Rewards.RepairVehicle`, `Challenges.Rewards.TeamOffence.Forgive`, `Challenges.Rewards.TeamOffence.Punish.Kill`, `Challenges.Rewards.Tip.Heal`, `Challenges.Rewards.Tip.PlayerDelivered`, `Challenges.Rewards.Tip.Refuel`, `Challenges.Rewards.Tip.Repair`, `Challenges.Rewards.Tip.Revive`, `Challenges.Rewards.VehicleDestroyed`, `Challenges.Rewards.VehicleDestroyed.Assist.CrewAssist`, `Challenges.Rewards.VehicleDestroyed.Assist.FOBSuppliedAssist`, `Challenges.Rewards.VehicleDestroyed.Assist.LogisticAssist`, `Challenges.Rewards.VehicleDestroyed.Assist.ReviveAssist`, `Challenges.Rewards.ZoneEntry.SpawnVehicle`) and 5 other tags (`Meta.Progression.Resources.MatchScore.BuildDamaged`, `Meta.Progression.Resources.MatchScore.DestroyedDeployable`, `Meta.Progression.Resources.MatchScore.SchematicPlacedInFOBScore`, `Meta.Progression.Resources.MatchScore.Vehicle.CrewAssist.PlayerKill`, `Meta.Progression.Resources.MatchScore.Vehicle.CrewAssist.VehicleKill`). Several `Challenges.Rewards.*` ones are the parent tag of a family (the lookup tables supply variant tags); others (e.g. `ZoneEntry.SpawnVehicle`, `TeamOffence.*`) have no formula row at all. Whether the game pays anything for a tag without a row is not traced.

<!-- /challenge_tables -->

## 7. Reward lookups used by the trees

Some trees do not name one reward tag. They hold a `Lookup Reward Tag for Variant` node with a registry name, filter tags (the key families it accepts) and an optional fallback tag. The table joins each registry to the lookup table of the same name and checks that every tag in that table has a formula row. **Data**; the registry-to-table step is **Derived** from names (`DestroyedBuildable` is matched to `BuildableDestroyed`).

<!-- challenge_tables lookups -->

<!-- /challenge_tables -->

- **Derived.** Only the base, `_Assist` and `SupplyCrateAssist` vehicle trees contain a lookup node, and they read the plain `DestroyVehicle` or `DestroyVehicleSection` registry. The `CrewAssist`, `LogisticAssist`, `ReviveAssist` and `FOBSuppliedAssist` trees have no lookup node, so the lookup tables with those names are not referenced by any tree registry name. Their reward tags (`VehicleDestroyed.LogisticAssist...`) have formula rows, so a different step probably builds the tag from the base one. Not traced.
- **Derived.** Every row in a lookup table that a tree uses has a formula row, so none of those lookups points at a tag missing from the formula tables.

## 11. Not traced

- **Native.** Nothing on this page is read from the game code (the reward evaluator is read in [round 88](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/docs/evidence/round88-nb.md)). What raises `Event.StateTree.PlayerKill`, `Event.StateTree.DestroyVehicle` and the rest, the `Calculate Reward` and `Grant Reward` tasks, and the schema class `WDChallengeStateTreeSchema` are not decompiled here.
- The cash tables under `CashRewardsDataTable` use `Meta.Progression.Context.*` and `Vehicle.Variant.*` keys, not `Challenges.Rewards.*` tags. Whether a `Calculate Reward` node reads them is not traced (some formulas call their base value a game value, `CalculatedReward`). The formula rows carry their own literal base values, and those are the only amounts joined on this page.
- Trees whose reward tag is bound at run time (for example `PlayerKill`) cannot be joined; section 6 lists them.
- Wager target values, the reward-feed text (`DT_ChallengesFeedActions*`) and the curve tables (`DT_Challenges_Curves_*`) are not covered here.
- Prerequisite meaning, the order in which several trees listening to related events fire, and the effect of auto-activate set to no (for example `BuildableDamaged` and `SchematicPlaced`) are not traced.
